The unofficial predecessor of this competition was the Fair Cities Cup, which was played from 1955 to 1971. From that season, the competition was officially called the UEFA Cup and was under that name until 2009, when it was renamed the UEFA Europa League. Over the years, the format of the competition has changed several times, and since the 2004/2005 season. a group stage was introduced followed by eliminations.
